```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
import heapq
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_fuzzy_state = None
_interval_state = None


def c5_eval(h, dx):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if h.size == 0:
        return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e100
    vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * float(dx)
    return float(np.max(np.maximum(vals, 0.0)))


def project_to_sum(y, target):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = y.size
    target = float(target)
    if n == 0:
        return y
    if target <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target >= float(n):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=float)

    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 2.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 2.0
    for _ in range(80):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if val > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    z = np.clip(y - lam, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(z.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        mask = (z > 1e-12) & (z < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            z[mask] += diff / float(mask.sum())
        else:
            if diff > 0.0:
                order = np.argsort(-z)
                for idx in order:
                    add = min(1.0 - float(z[idx]), diff)
                    if add <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    z[idx] += add
                    diff -= add
                    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
                        break
            else:
                order = np.argsort(z)
                ndiff = -diff
                for idx in order:
                    sub = min(float(z[idx]), ndiff)
                    if sub <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    z[idx] -= sub
                    ndiff -= sub
                    if ndiff < 1e-14:
                        break
    return np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)


def ensure_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if h.size < 2:
        h = np.zeros(2, dtype=float)
    if h.size % 2 == 1:
        h = h[:h.size - 1]
    target = float(h.size // 2)
    if abs(float(h.sum()) - target) > 1e-8:
        h = project_to_sum(h, target)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-11:
        h = project_to_sum(h, target)
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def make_even(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size < 2:
        return np.zeros(2, dtype=float)
    if h.size % 2 == 1:
        h = h[:h.size - 1]
    return ensure_h(h)


def round_to_sum(h, n_one):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    k = int(round(float(n_one)))
    if k < 0:
        k = 0
    if k > n:
        k = n
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k > 0:
        order = np.argsort(-h, kind="mergesort")
        b[order[:k]] = 1.0
    return b


def softmax_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    a = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(a)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / float(z.size))
    return e / s


def top_weights_vector(vals, M):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    L = vals.size
    weights = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if L == 0 or M <= 0:
        return weights
    M = int(min(M, L))
    if M >= L:
        order = np.arange(L)
    else:
        order = np.argpartition(vals, -M)[-M:]
    top = vals[order]
    if top.size == 0:
        return weights
    spread = float(top.max() - top.min())
    T = max(1e-6, 0.75 * spread if spread > 1e-8 else 0.005)
    w = np.exp((top - float(top.max())) / T)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        weights[order] = w / s
    else:
        weights[order] = 1.0 / float(top.size)
    return weights


def weighted_gradient(h, weights, dx):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = h.size
    if n == 0:
        return h.copy()
    mid = n - 1
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if mid < weights.size:
        w0 = float(weights[mid])
        if w0 != 0.0:
            grad += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
    dx = float(dx)
    for d in range(1, n):
        ip = mid - d
        im = mid + d
        wp = float(weights[ip]) if 0 <= ip < weights.size else 0.0
        wm = float(weights[im]) if 0 <= im < weights.size else 0.0
        if wp == 0.0 and wm == 0.0:
            continue
        m = n - d
        if wp != 0.0:
            wpdx = wp * dx
            grad[:m] += wpdx * (1.0 - h[d:])
            grad[d:] += -wpdx * h[:m]
        if wm != 0.0:
            wmdx = wm * dx
            grad[:m] += -wmdx * h[d:]
            grad[d:] += wmdx * (1.0 - h[:m])
    return grad


def select_pairs_by_gradient(grad, h, K):
    n = h.size
    if n < 2:
        return []
    K = int(max(0, K))
    if K == 0:
        return []

    if n > 1000:
        heap = []
        for i in range(n):
            gi = float(grad[i])
            hi = float(h[i])
            for j in range(i + 1, n):
                gj = float(grad[j])
                hj = float(h[j])
                if gi > gj:
                    amount = min(hi, 1.0 - hj)
                    D = -amount
                else:
                    amount = min(1.0 - hi, hj)
                    D = amount
                if amount > 1e-9:
                    score = abs(gi - gj) * amount
                    item = (-score, i, j, D)
                    if len(heap) < K:
                        heapq.heappush(heap, item)
                    else:
                        if score > -heap[0][0]:
                            heapq.heapreplace(heap, item)
        return [(int(i), int(j), float(D)) for (_, i, j, D) in heap]

    diff = grad[:, None] - grad[None, :]
    hi = h[:, None]
    hj = h[None, :]
    use_i = diff > 0.0
    amount = np.minimum(hi, 1.0 - hj) * use_i + np.minimum(1.0 - hi, hj) * (1.0 - use_i)
    sign = np.where(use_i, -1.0, 1.0)
    D = sign * amount
    score = np.abs(diff) * amount
    np.fill_diagonal(score, 0.0)

    flat = score.ravel()
    if flat.size == 0:
        return []
    K = min(K, flat.size)
    if K == 0:
        return []
    if K >= flat.size:
        idxs = np.flatnonzero(flat > 1e-15)
        if idxs.size:
            idxs = idxs[np.argsort(-flat[idxs])]
    else:
        idxs = np.argpartition(flat, -K)[-K:]
        sel = flat[idxs]
        idxs = idxs[np.argsort(-sel)]

    out = []
    for flat_idx in idxs:
        i = int(flat_idx // n)
        j = int(flat_idx % n)
        if i == j:
            continue
        dval = float(D[i, j])
        if abs(dval) > 1e-9:
            out.append((i, j, dval))
        if len(out) >= K:
            break
    return out


def pair_best_move(h, i, j, D, current_c, dx, refine_counts=7):
    if abs(D) < 1e-10:
        return None, current_c
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    best_h = None
    best_c = current_c
    quick = (0.5, 1.0, 0.25)
    for f in quick:
        delta = D * float(f)
        nh = h.copy()
        nh[i] += delta
        nh[j] -= delta
        if nh[i] < -1e-11 or nh[i] > 1.0 + 1e-11 or nh[j] < -1e-11 or nh[j] > 1.0 + 1e-11:
            continue
        c = c5_eval(nh, dx)
        if c < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c
            best_h = nh.copy()
            best_f = float(f)

    if best_h is not None and refine_counts > 0:
        a = max(0.0, best_f - 0.35)
        b = min(1.0, best_f + 0.35)
        grid = np.linspace(a, b, max(3, int(refine_counts)))
        for f in grid:
            ff = float(f)
            if abs(ff - best_f) < 1e-5:
                continue
            delta = D * ff
            nh = h.copy()
            nh[i] += delta
            nh[j] -= delta
            if nh[i] < -1e-11 or nh[i] > 1.0 + 1e-11 or nh[j] < -1e-11 or nh[j] > 1.0 + 1e-11:
                continue
            c = c5_eval(nh, dx)
            if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = c
                best_h = nh.copy()
                best_f = ff
    return best_h, best_c


def projected_gradient_step(h, c, dx, weights, N, phase_deadline=None):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    g = weighted_gradient(h, weights, dx)
    g = g - float(np.mean(g))
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
    if scale < 1e-12:
        return h, c
    direction = g / scale
    taus = (0.01, 0.03, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0)
    best_h = None
    best_c = c
    for tau in taus:
        if phase_deadline is not None and time.time() > phase_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        y = h - float(tau) * direction
        yh = project_to_sum(y, float(N))
        ch = c5_eval(yh, dx)
        if ch < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_h = yh.copy()
            best_c = ch
    return best_h, best_c


def random_hill_climb(h, rng, dx, max_steps, phase_deadline, temperature=0.0008):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    c = c5_eval(h, dx)
    n = h.size
    if n < 2:
        return h, c
    for _ in range(int(max_steps)):
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 2.0:
            break
        high = np.flatnonzero(h > 0.5 + 1e-4)
        low = np.flatnonzero(h < 0.5 - 1e-4)
        if high.size and low.size:
            i = int(rng.choice(high))
            j = int(rng.choice(low))
        else:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            if i == j:
                continue
        if float(h[i]) > float(h[j]):
            amount = min(float(h[i]), 1.0 - float(h[j]))
            D = -amount
        else:
            amount = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(h[j]))
            D = amount
        if abs(D) < 1e-9:
            continue
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0))
        nh = h.copy()
        nh[i] += D * f
        nh[j] -= D * f
        nc = c5_eval(nh, dx)
        if nc < c:
            h = nh
            c = nc
        elif temperature > 0.0:
            diff = nc - c
            if diff > 0.0:
                logp = -diff / temperature
                if logp > -700.0:
                    if rng.random() < math.exp(logp):
                        h = nh
                        c = nc
        else:
            pass
    return h, c


def coordinate_polish(h, rng, phase_deadline):
    h = make_even(h)
    n = h.size
    N = n // 2
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    c = c5_eval(h, dx)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c

    M = int(min(80, max(32, n // 4)))
    K = int(max(50, min(300, 200000 // max(1, n))))
    refine_counts = 9 if n <= 256 else 5
    no_improve = 0

    while time.time() < phase_deadline - 15.0:
        vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
        vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
        weights = top_weights_vector(vals, M)

        gh, gc = projected_gradient_step(h, c, dx, weights, N, phase_deadline)
        if gc < c - 1e-12:
            h = gh
            c = gc
            no_improve = 0
            if c < best_c:
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c
            continue

        grad = weighted_gradient(h, weights, dx)
        pairs = select_pairs_by_gradient(grad, h, K)
        moved = False
        moves = 0
        for (i, j, D) in pairs:
            if time.time() > phase_deadline - 15.0:
                break
            nh, nc = pair_best_move(h, i, j, D, c, dx, refine_counts)
            if nh is not None and nc < c - 1e-12:
                h = nh
                c = nc
                moved = True
                moves += 1
                if c < best_c:
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c
                if moves >= 8:
                    break

        if moved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            hp, cp = random_hill_climb(h, rng, dx, 40, phase_deadline, 0.0008)
            if cp < c + 0.003 and cp < best_c + 0.01:
                h = hp
                c = cp
                no_improve = 0
                if c < best_c:
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve > 4:
                    break

    if time.time() < phase_deadline - 1.0:
        cc = c5_eval(best_h, dx)
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
    return best_h, best_c


def interval_params_to_bounds(raw, K):
    a = softmax_z(raw[:K])
    g = softmax_z(raw[K:])
    s = np.empty(K, dtype=float)
    e = np.empty(K, dtype=float)
    pos = float(g[0])
    for j in range(int(K)):
        s[j] = pos
        ee = pos + float(a[j])
        e[j] = ee
        pos = ee + float(g[j + 1])
    return s, e


class IntervalState:
    def __init__(self, K, n_t):
        self.K = int(K)
        self.n_t = int(n_t)
        self.t = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, self.n_t, endpoint=True)
        self.two = 2.0 - self.t

    def c_true(self, raw):
        K = self.K
        s, e = interval_params_to_bounds(raw, K)
        t = self.t
        two = self.two
        n_t = self.n_t
        P = np.zeros(n_t, dtype=float)
        R = np.zeros(n_t, dtype=float)
        Q = np.zeros(n_t, dtype=float)

        for j in range(K):
            sj = float(s[j])
            ej = float(e[j])
            P += np.maximum(0.0, np.minimum(ej, two) - sj)

        for el_idx in range(K):
            sl = float(s[el_idx])
            el = float(e[el_idx])
            low = np.maximum(sl - t, 0.0)
            up = np.minimum(el - t, two)
            R += np.maximum(0.0, up - low)

        for j in range(K):
            sj = float(s[j])
            ej = float(e[j])
            for l in range(K):
                sl = float(s[l])
                el = float(e[l])
                low = np.maximum(sj, sl + t)
                up = np.minimum(ej, el + t)
                up = np.minimum(up, two)
                Q += np.maximum(0.0, up - low)

        vals = np.maximum(P - Q, R - Q)
        vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
        return float(np.max(vals))

    def objective(self, raw):
        return self.c_true(raw)


def _interval_obj(raw):
    return _interval_state.objective(raw)


def optimize_interval_K(K, rng, phase_deadline):
    global _interval_state
    K = int(K)
    if K < 1:
        K = 1
    n_t = 500 if K >= 5 else 600
    _interval_state = IntervalState(K, n_t)

    psize = 2 * K + 1
    raw_best = None
    c_best = 1e100

    raws = [np.zeros(psize, dtype=float)]
    for _ in range(2):
        raws.append(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, psize))

    for raw0 in raws:
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 12.0:
            break
        raw = raw0.copy()
        c0 = _interval_state.c_true(raw)
        if c0 < c_best:
            c_best = c0
            raw_best = raw.copy()
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _interval_obj,
                raw.copy(),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 120, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-8},
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                c = float(res.fun)
                if not math.isfinite(c):
                    c = _interval_state.c_true(res.x)
                if c < c_best:
                    c_best = c
                    raw_best = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if raw_best is None:
        raw_best = raws[0].copy()
    return raw_best, c_best


def interval_to_h(raw, K, n):
    raw = np.asarray(raw, dtype=float)
    K = int(K)
    n = int(n)
    s, e = interval_params_to_bounds(raw, K)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * dx
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for j in range(K):
        h[(x >= float(s[j])) & (x < float(e[j]))] = 1.0

    N = n // 2
    diff = float(N - float(h.sum()))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-8 and K > 0:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idxs = np.flatnonzero(h == 0.0)
            set_val = 1.0
        else:
            idxs = np.flatnonzero(h == 1.0)
            set_val = 0.0
        if idxs.size:
            eps = np.concatenate((s, e))
            dist = np.min(np.abs(x[idxs, None] - eps[None, :]), axis=1)
            take = int(round(abs(diff)))
            take = min(take, len(idxs))
            if take > 0:
                order = idxs[np.argsort(dist)][:take]
                h[order] = set_val

    diff2 = float(N - float(h.sum()))
    if abs(diff2) > 1e-8:
        h = project_to_sum(h, float(N))
    return h


def run_interval_search(rng, phase_deadline, current_best_c):
    global _interval_state
    remain = phase_deadline - time.time()
    if remain < 30.0:
        return None, 1e100

    n = 512 if remain > 180.0 else (384 if remain > 90.0 else 256)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n -= 1
    if n < 128:
        n = 128

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100
    for K in (5, 4, 6, 3):
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 30.0:
            break
        raw, c_cont = optimize_interval_K(K, rng, phase_deadline)
        if raw is None:
            continue
        if c_cont < max(0.388, current_best_c + 0.008):
            h = interval_to_h(raw, K, n)
            h = ensure_h(h)
            c = c5_eval(h, 2.0 / float(n))
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


class FuzzyState:
    def __init__(self, b, free, target, dx, Tlog, P):
        self.b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
        self.free = np.asarray(free, dtype=int)
        self.target = float(target)
        self.dx = float(dx)
        self.Tlog = float(Tlog)
        self.P = float(P)
        self.k = int(self.free.size)
        self._last_y = None
        self._last_obj = None
        self._last_grad = None

    def x_from_y(self, y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        x = np.empty(self.k, dtype=float)
        if self.k <= 0:
            x = np.array([self.target], dtype=float)
            return x
        if self.k == 1:
            x[0] = self.target
            return x
        x[:-1] = y
        x[-1] = self.target - float(y.sum())
        return x

    def compute(self, y):
        x = self.x_from_y(y)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros(self.k, dtype=float)

        x_use = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
        if abs(float(x_use.sum()) - self.target) > 1e-7 or bool(np.any(x < -1e-8)) or bool(np.any(x > 1.0 + 1e-8)):
            x_use = project_to_sum(x, self.target)

        h = self.b.copy()
        if self.k and self.free.size:
            h[self.free] = x_use

        vals = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * self.dx
        vals = np.maximum(vals, 0.0)
        if vals.size == 0:
            m = 0.0
            weights = np.zeros(0)
        else:
            m = float(vals.max())
            w = np.exp((vals - m) / self.Tlog)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                weights = w / s
            else:
                weights = np.full(vals.size, 1.0 / vals.size)
        obj = m + float(self.Tlog) * math.log(float(w.sum()) if vals.size else 1.0)

        grad_h = weighted_gradient(h, weights, self.dx)
        below = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
        above = np.maximum(x - 1.0, 0.0)
        pen = self.P * (float(np.sum(below * below)) + float(np.sum(above * above)))
        grad_x = grad_h[self.free].copy()
        grad_x += self.P * (-2.0 * below + 2.0 * above)
        return obj + pen, grad_x

    def objective(self, y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if self._last_y is not None and np.array_equal(y, self._last_y):
            return self._last_obj
        obj, grad = self.compute(y)
        self._last_y = y.copy()
        self._last_obj = obj
        self._last_grad = grad
        return obj

    def gradient(self, y):
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
        if self._last_y is not None and np.array_equal(y, self._last_y):
            return self._last_grad
        obj, grad = self.compute(y)
        self._last_y = y.copy()
        self._last_obj = obj
        self._last_grad = grad
        return grad


def _fuzzy_fun(y):
    return _fuzzy_state.objective(y)


def _fuzzy_jac(y):
    return _fuzzy_state.gradient(y)


def choose_free_indices(h, b, K):
    n = h.size
    K = int(min(K, n))
    if K <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=int)
    score = np.minimum(h, 1.0 - h) + 0.5 * np.abs(h - b)
    order = np.argsort(-score, kind="mergesort")
    return order[:K]


def fuzzy_polish(h, rng, phase_deadline):
    global _fuzzy_state
    h = make_even(h)
    n = h.size
    N = n // 2
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c5_eval(h, dx)
    if n < 10 or n > 512:
        return best_h, best_c

    full_temps = (0.02, 0.008, 0.003)
    for Tlog in full_temps:
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 25.0:
            break
        start = best_h.copy()
        if Tlog < 0.01 and n <= 512:
            start = start + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01, n)
            start = ensure_h(start)
        free = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
        target = float(N)
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        x0 = project_to_sum(start[free], target)
        if n > 1:
            pivot = int(np.argmax(np.minimum(x0, 1.0 - x0)))
            perm = np.concatenate((np.arange(0, pivot), np.arange(pivot + 1, n), np.array([pivot], dtype=int)))
            free = free[perm]
            x0 = x0[perm]
        _fuzzy_state = FuzzyState(b, free, target, dx, Tlog, 1e5)
        y0 = x0[:-1].copy()
        k = free.size
        if k < 2:
            continue
        maxiter = 80 if n <= 256 else 40
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _fuzzy_fun,
                y0,
                jac=_fuzzy_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=[(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(k - 1)],
                options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "maxfun": int(8 * maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-10},
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                xres = _fuzzy_state.x_from_y(res.x)
                xres = project_to_sum(xres, target)
                hnew = b.copy()
                hnew[free] = xres
                cnew = c5_eval(hnew, dx)
                if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hnew.copy()
                    best_c = cnew
        except Exception:
            pass

    for K in (4, 8, 16, 32, 64):
        if time.time() > phase_deadline - 25.0:
            break
        if K > n:
            continue
        if K < 2:
            continue
        b = round_to_sum(best_h, N)
        free = choose_free_indices(best_h, b, K)
        target = float(b[free].sum())
        if target <= 1e-12 or target >= float(K) - 1e-12:
            continue
        x0 = project_to_sum(best_h[free], target)
        if x0.size > 1:
            pivot = int(np.argmax(np.minimum(x0, 1.0 - x0)))
            perm = np.concatenate((np.arange(0, pivot), np.arange(pivot + 1, K), np.array([pivot], dtype=int)))
            free = free[perm]
            x0 = x0[perm]
        _fuzzy_state = FuzzyState(b, free, target, dx, 0.008, 1e5)
        y0 = x0[:-1].copy()
        k = free.size
        if k < 2:
            continue
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _fuzzy_fun,
                y0,
                jac=_fuzzy_jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=[(0.0, 1.0) for _ in range(k - 1)],
                options={"maxiter": 40, "maxfun": 320, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-10},
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                xres = _fuzzy_state.x_from_y(res.x)
                xres = project_to_sum(xres, target)
                hnew = b.copy()
                hnew[free] = xres
                cnew = c5_eval(hnew, dx)
                if cnew < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_h = hnew.copy()
                    best_c = cnew
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def structured_start(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n -= 1
    if n < 64:
        n = 64
    N = n // 2
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    score = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 11, 13):
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.283185307179586))
        score += np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * float(f) * x + ph) / float(f)
    order = np.argsort(-score, kind="mergesort")
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    h[order[:N]] = 1.0
    return h


def finalize_h(h):
    h = make_even(h)
    return h


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    global _fuzzy_state
    global _interval_state

    t_start = time.time()
    deadline = t_start + float(budget_s)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100
    best_n = 0

    try:
        arr = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        h0 = make_even(arr.copy())
    except Exception:
        h0 = structured_start(240, rng)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_n = h0.size
    best_c = c5_eval(best_h, 2.0 / float(best_n))

    scale = min(1.0, max(0.2, float(budget_s) / 900.0))

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 40.0:
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 180.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 5.0:
            h1, c1 = coordinate_polish(best_h, rng, phase)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h = h1
                best_c = c1
                best_n = h1.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 30.0:
        hb = round_to_sum(best_h, best_n // 2)
        cb = c5_eval(hb, 2.0 / float(best_n))
        if cb < best_c:
            best_h = hb
            best_c = cb
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 80.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 5.0:
            h2, c2 = coordinate_polish(hb, rng, phase)
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
                best_n = h2.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 70.0 and best_n <= 512:
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 140.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 10.0:
            h3, c3 = fuzzy_polish(best_h, rng, phase)
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h = h3
                best_c = c3
                best_n = h3.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 150.0:
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 260.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 30.0:
            hi, ci = run_interval_search(rng, phase, best_c)
            if hi is not None:
                if ci < best_c:
                    best_h = hi
                    best_c = ci
                    best_n = hi.size
                if ci < best_c + 0.01 and (deadline - time.time()) > 60.0:
                    phase2 = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + min(90.0 * scale, 120.0))
                    if phase2 > time.time() + 10.0:
                        h4, c4 = coordinate_polish(hi, rng, phase2)
                        if c4 < best_c:
                            best_h = h4
                            best_c = c4
                            best_n = h4.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 90.0 and best_n <= 512:
        new_n = best_n * 2
        if new_n % best_n == 0 and new_n <= 512 and new_n >= 128:
            h_up = np.repeat(best_h, new_n // best_n)
            h_up = ensure_h(h_up)
            c_up = c5_eval(h_up, 2.0 / float(h_up.size))
            if c_up < best_c + 0.02:
                phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 100.0 * scale)
                if phase > time.time() + 10.0:
                    h5, c5v = coordinate_polish(h_up, rng, phase)
                    if c5v < best_c:
                        best_h = h5
                        best_c = c5v
                        best_n = h5.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 120.0 and best_n <= 512:
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 130.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 15.0:
            h6, c6 = fuzzy_polish(best_h, rng, phase)
            if c6 < best_c:
                best_h = h6
                best_c = c6
                best_n = h6.size

    remain = deadline - time.time()
    if remain > 60.0:
        nt = 256 if best_n < 256 else min(512, max(best_n, 256))
        if nt % 2 == 1:
            nt -= 1
        hs = structured_start(nt, rng)
        phase = min(deadline - 15.0, time.time() + 80.0 * scale)
        if phase > time.time() + 10.0:
            h7, c7 = coordinate_polish(hs, rng, phase)
            if c7 < best_c:
                best_h = h7
                best_c = c7
                best_n = h7.size

    h = finalize_h(best_h)
    n = int(h.size)
    c = c5_eval(h, 2.0 / float(n))

    if n % 2 == 0:
        snap = round_to_sum(h, n // 2)
        cs = c5_eval(snap, 2.0 / float(n))
        if cs <= c + 1e-15:
            h = snap
            c = cs

    return h, float(c), int(n)
```
